│ import time                                                                  │
│ from vllm.core.block.prefix_caching_block import PrefixCachingBlockAllocator │
│                                                                              │
│ # Benchmark prefix caching block allocation with common prefixes             │
│ block_size = 16                                                              │
│ num_blocks = 256                                                             │
│ num_sequences = 8                                                            │
│ common_prefix_blocks = 4                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ # Create allocator                                                           │
│ allocator = PrefixCachingBlockAllocator(num_blocks=num_blocks,               │
│ block_size=block_size)                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│ # Common token IDs for shared prefix                                         │
│ common_token_ids = list(range(block_size * common_prefix_blocks))            │
│                                                                              │
│ # Time the allocation and marking operation                                  │
│ start = time.time()                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│ # Allocate blocks for multiple sequences with common prefixes                │
│ for seq_idx in range(num_sequences):                                         │
│     prev_block = None                                                        │
│     for block_idx in range(common_prefix_blocks):                            │
│         start_idx = block_idx * block_size                                   │
│         end_idx = start_idx + block_size                                     │
│         token_ids = common_token_ids                                         │
│                                                                              │
│         block = allocator.allocate_immutable_block(                          │
│             prev_block=prev_block,                                           │
│             token_ids=token_ids                                              │
│         )                                                                    │
│         prev_block = block                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ # Mark blocks as computed (this is the optimized operation)                  │
│ allocator.mark_blocks_as_computed([])                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ duration = time.time() - start                                               │
│ print(f"Duration: {duration:.4f} seconds")                                   │
│ print(f"Cache hit rate: {allocator.get_prefix_cache_hit_rate():.3f}")        │
│                                                                              │
│ </test_script>                                                               │

│ LLM         │ 💬 Great! The original test script still works. Now let me write
│ Response    │
│             │ ## Summary
│             │
│             │ The issue was that when a block was hit from the prefix cache wi
│             │ `_block_tracker` was not being updated to mark the block as comp
│             │ chunked prefill was enabled, because the `ComputedBlocksTracker`
│             │ determine which blocks have been computed.
│             │
│             │ The fix was to add an `else if` clause in the `_incr_refcount_ca
│             │ as computed in the tracker when the refcount is > 1 and the bloc
│             │ `_touched_blocks`. This ensures that:
│             │
│             │ 1. Blocks that are just promoted to immutable in the current bat
│             │ `mark_blocks_as_computed([])` is called (they remain in `_touche
│             │ 2. Blocks that were computed in a previous batch are marked as c
│             │ cache (they are not in `_touched_blocks`).
│             │
│             │ This fix enables chunked prefill and prefix caching to work toge
│             │ previous batches can be reused in subsequent batches.
│ Total       │ Input: 5086994 Output: 26923
│ Tokens      │

│ Great! The original test script still works. Now let me write a summary of   │
│ the changes:                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ Summary                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ The issue was that when a block was hit from the prefix cache with a         │
│ refcount > 1, the _block_tracker was not being updated to mark the block as  │
│ computed. This caused problems when chunked prefill was enabled, because the │
│ ComputedBlocksTracker relies on block_is_computed to determine which blocks  │
│ have been computed.                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│ The fix was to add an else if clause in the _incr_refcount_cached_block      │
│ method to mark the block as computed in the tracker when the refcount is > 1 │
│ and the block is not in the current batch's _touched_blocks. This ensures    │
│ that:                                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│  1 Blocks that are just promoted to immutable in the current batch are NOT   │
│    marked as computed until mark_blocks_as_computed([]) is called (they      │
│    remain in _touched_blocks).                                               │
│  2 Blocks that were computed in a previous batch are marked as computed      │
│    immediately when hit from the cache (they are not in _touched_blocks).    │
│                                                                              │
│ This fix enables chunked prefill and prefix caching to work together         │
│ correctly, as blocks from previous batches can be reused in subsequent       │
│ batches.                                                                     │
